## §ccxxxiv FillLightning — port verified faithful; residual is upstream input data, not tree ops

Exhaustive line-by-line re-verification of `fill/lightning/*` against upstream `TreeNode.cpp`/`Layer.cpp`/`Generator.cpp`/`DistanceField.cpp` confirms the port is **faithful**, including the subtle ownership semantics:

- `deep_copy` == `TreeNode.cpp:91` (`is_root` + `last_grounding.value_or(m_p)`, parent set to `local_root`).
- `reroot` == `TreeNode.cpp:109` (recursive old-parent re-root, `new_parent` remove+`m_is_root=false`, else `is_root=true`+`parent.reset()`).
- `reconnectRoots` == `Layer.cpp:220` verbatim incl. BOTH re-grounding paths: grounding-path `addChild(new_root); new_root->reroot()` (243-255) AND boundary-path `Node::create(ground.p); attach_ptr->reroot(); new_root->addChild(attach_ptr)` (271-281) — the depth-growing behavior is ported.
- `propagate_to_next_layer` == `TreeNode.cpp:51` (deepCopy→prune→straighten→realign→push).
- `DistanceField::new` == `DistanceField.cpp:37` (cell_size=radius/6, union_ex+offset2_ex dangling-line removal, `Line::distance_to_squared`, stable_sort by `|b.dist-a.dist|>radius ? dist : PointHash%191`).

Since tree ops are verbatim, the complex-object residual (ares 1 sparse vs oracle 38) is **upstream of the tree logic** — in the `infill_outlines`/`overhang`/`unsupported` surface classification feeding `generateTrees`, or in `current_outlines` propagation. Confirmed: plain cube emits 38/38 (matching oracle) — logic works when overhang data is simple; complex geometry's overhang/outline inputs diverge earlier in the pipeline (surface-type detection or bridge-over-infill expansion), before lightning trees are involved.

Needs: oracle-side dump of `overhang_per_layer`/`layer_outlines` (not just tree coords) to localize which input layer set differs. Track as known-residual; not a `fill/lightning` bug.

## §ccxxxv FillLightning — concrete residual lead: `layer_internal_outlines` source coverage

Upstream `Generator.cpp:184-193` builds `infill_outlines[layer_id]` by iterating `print_object.get_layer(layer_id)->regions()` — **every LayerRegion's** `fill_surfaces` filtered to `stInternal|stInternalVoid`, on EVERY layer (no Option gap).

Ares `build_lightning_generator` (`candidate_expansion.rs:270-283`) instead reads `horizontal.records[layer_id]` — `PreparedSurfaceTypeObject.records: Vec<Option<PreparedSurfaceTypeRecord>>` — and `unwrap_or_default()` → empty on layers where the record is `None`. If `records` is sparse on complex objects (a record only where horizontal-shell/surface-type work produced one), `infill_outlines` is empty for those layers → `generateTrees` sees no overhang/no outline → trees don't propagate → sparse collapse (ares 1 vs oracle 38 on complex obj; cube's records happen to be dense → 38/38).

ALSO: `records[layer_id].fill_surfaces` may hold a single merged record vs upstream's per-region union — same net polygons if merge is faithful, but coverage gap (None layers) is the suspect.

Next diagnostic: dump `horizontal.records.iter().map(|r| r.is_some())` + `layer_internal_outlines.iter().map(Vec::len)` per layer for the complex case; compare against oracle `infill_outlines` poly counts. If gaps exist, source `infill_outlines` from the full per-layer region fill_surfaces (upstream `layerm->fill_surfaces`) instead of the horizontal-record subset.

## §ccxxxvi FillLightning — independent-review fixes verified: 1→26 sparse (oracle 38)

The reviewer-identified lightning defects were the real residual drivers (not the §ccxxxii "straighten" hypothesis nor the §ccxxxv records-gap hypothesis — `rec_some` is all `true`, no coverage gap). Fixed+verified on complex object `parity-lnN/case-IdM3Br`: sparse sections **1 → 26** (oracle 38).

Confirmed-causal fixes:
- `LOCATOR_CELL_SIZE` `4000` → `4_000_000` (`scaled<coord_t>(4.)`, `TreeNode.hpp:21`) — the 1000×-too-small locator grid broke `getBestGroundingLocation`/`lineSegmentPolygonsIntersection` reach.
- `reconnectRoots` grounding path — `addChild(new_root); new_root->reroot()` subtree-preserve (was `reroot(new_root, Some(root_ptr))` which dropped the tree).
- `straighten_rec` — integer-truncate `junction_moving_dir`/`m_p +=` arithmetic (was f64+`.round()`), and live-`m_p` collinear test (was stale pre-move `p`).
- `m_supporting_radius` — float division `coord_t(w)*100*n/density` (was int division).
- `Generator` params — `max` nozzle diameter + `object_config.layer_height` (was `min` + first-layer height).
- Shared `GlibcRand` stream across `convertToPolylines`/layers (was per-layer reseed).
- `multiline_fill` applied before the 2nd `intersection_pl` (was skipped).

Remaining 26→38 residual: propagated trees arrive at lower layers as **root-only single nodes** (`children=[]` → `raw_lines=0` on layers 5-41; only seed layer 42 has children). Hypothesis: upstream `reconnectRoots` boundary re-grounding builds deeper root→…→unsupported chains that survive `propagate`'s prune+realign on shrinking outlines; ares's propagated 2-node tree loses its single child when the child's overhang position falls outside the smaller next-layer outline. Needs oracle-side per-layer `tree_roots` depth dump to disambiguate which upstream step preserves trunk depth.

## §ccxxxviii Round-3 review fix ledger

Applied (commit `b48d38a6`, option_coverage 15/15 green):
- **P0 regression fix** — `distance_field.rs` `unsupported_points.sort_by` used
  `b.abs() - a.abs()` (abs-per-operand) AND a `Vec::sort_by` total-order-checked
  sort on a comparator upstream keeps non-transitive (`DistanceField.cpp:72-77`
  `stable_sort`). Now: `abs(b.dist - a.dist)` (abs of the difference) via
  `fixed_gcc_sort_by` (GCC `stable_sort` emulation tolerating the upstream
  non-strict-weak-order). Resolves the `smallsort` total-order panic that the
  round-3 width/tie fixes triggered on the complex object.
- **Lightning boundary-tie** — `get_best_grounding_location`: a tree candidate
  tied with the INITIAL boundary distance can no longer win (strict `<` for the
  first candidate, upstream `Layer.cpp:171` `candidate_dist < local_current_dist`);
  grid-addr tie-break only ranks already-winning candidates.
- **`m_infill_extrusion_width`** — now `scaled<float>` scale-domain f32
  (`f32(0.42*1e6)=420000.0`, not the `coord_t`-truncated 419999), with the
  upstream `EPSILON` fallback to `line_width.get_abs_value` or
  `Flow::auto_extrusion_width` (1.125*nozzle). `Generator::new` takes
  `sparse`/`line_width`/`nozzle` f64 `get_abs_value` args.
- **Anchoring multiline re-clip** — `intersection_pl` after `multiline_fill`
